Raw evidence: _evidence/husband-serp-verification-2026-08-04.json — every ranking URL, position, peer hit and autocomplete response.

1. The two findings that change the plan

Finding 1 — there are competitors we did not know about, and they outrank the ones we did.

Ollen Group (ollengroup.com, Dubai/UAE/KSA mall consultancy) ranks #1 or #2 on all four priority Middle East terms, above RLPC every time. It is in neither the dossier nor the 2 Aug competitor set. McArthur + Company (mcarthurcompany.com) ranks four times across the Dubai/Middle East terms. Both are direct structural peers — boutique, mall-specific, landlord-side. Also newly visible: The Mall Company, Kinetic Consultancy, SCG Consulting, mSquared (msquared.ae), and on the UK asset-management term, Sovereign Centros, IM Groups and Scoop AM.

Finding 2 — the list is phrased below Google's autocomplete threshold.

Nineteen of the twenty search terms return zero autocomplete suggestions. The instrument works — control queries returned suggestions ("shopping mall" → 10, "retail consultant" → 10, "shopping centre asset management" → 2, "mall repositioning" → 2). Shorter head phrasings clear the threshold; our long "…consultant/consultancy/advisory + geo" constructions do not. That is not proof of zero demand, but it is a real warning that this list is built almost entirely from terms too long to register. Confirming it needs the research tool pull that is still blocked.

  4. Do not commit the second production page to the Investment / Asset Management pillar. On this evidence the second page should be shopping mall feasibility study consultant — the one term where a comparable-size peer has proved the model with a dedicated page at #1.
  5. Free one Chrome profile before the next research session. Both browser tool profiles were locked by other sessions today, which is what forced the API route.

Standing rule applied throughout: no search term, volume, reading of the searcher's purpose or priority in this document is inferred. Every claim above traces to a results page pulled on 4 August 2026 and stored in the evidence file. Where a figure could not be sourced, it says so.

6. Demand check — added 10 August 2026, and it changes the plan

Source: the research tool, logged-in browser session, zero API units. Pulled 10 August 2026. Every figure below is worldwide with its country breakdown where the research tool provides one, so no market has been assumed and none has been missed.

Term Global volume Where it sits Competition (0–100)

"feasibility study" family, the tool's US records69,090 / month across 14,581 termsneither "mall" nor "retail" appears in the top 50 modifiershead term competition 48

For contrast — a term in the same sector that is alive: retail consultant, 3,500/month globally (US 590 · UK 390 · Portugal 320 · India 170 · Australia 110 · Germany 110), competition 19% "easy", cost per click £2.44. The sector has demand. Our phrasing does not reach it.

These are sourced answers, not blocked sources. The research tool returned "Volume 0, competition n/a" and "We have no data to show" across its trend, questions and related-terms panels — the signature of a genuinely dead term rather than an unreachable one.

What this overturns

  • §4's "Build first" ranking is void. It ranked terms on results page composition alone — who ranks and how well defended they are — with no demand figure to weigh against it. The #1 pick, shopping mall feasibility study consultant, has a dedicated peer page at position 1 and essentially nobody searching for it.
  • The autocomplete warning in §1 was correct. Nineteen of twenty terms below the suggestion threshold has now been confirmed by a second instrument.
  • The whole list is phrased too long. Head phrasings clear the threshold and carry volume; "…consultant / consultancy / advisory + geography" constructions do not. The list needs rebuilding from head terms downward, not the reverse.

What replaces it

Not a guess. The next research pass runs the house gate properly on candidate head terms — global volume with country breakdown, competition score, and a reading of what the searcher wants read off the live results page — before any term is ranked again. Candidates to test first, on the evidence above: retail consultant and its commercial variations (retail consulting, retail store consultant), and the same treatment for the leasing and asset-management pillars. What the searcher wants is the catch to watch: the research tool labels retail consultant "Informational" while labelling four of its own variations "Commercial", which is exactly why the results page, not the label, decides.

How to keep this file honest

  • Edit here, not in the originals. A number changed in SEO/husband-keyword-research-2026-08-11.md will not appear in this document.
  • Every tab keeps its own provenance line naming the file it came from, so any figure can be traced back to where it was first written.
  • Nothing was rewritten during the merge. Headings were pushed down one level so they nest correctly inside a tab, and each document's own title became its tab or section name. No wording, number or verdict was altered.
  • The originals can be retired once you are satisfied nothing was lost. That is a deletion, so it needs a word from you first.
